Embedding Geometry Diagnostics

Fixed-subset hidden-state diagnostics for trained NL-RX-SYNTH checkpoints. These plots answer whether the cap objective changes representation spread, clustering, and text-code pairing; they do not replace exact-match generation as the primary result.
